# Agents · D · part 2

909 entries, data-critic to data-scientist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **data-critic**: ライブモデレーター + データ検証ゲートキーパーの専門家。 Phase 1/2 のディスカッションをモデレート（矛盾検出・議論促進・収束判定）し、 Gate 1/2 で全員参加の合意形成をファシリテートする。 Gate 2 では Devil's Advocate ラウンドと Independent Validation を運営。 確証バイアスを排除するため「望ましい結論」を知らない状態で検証する。 コンテキストファイル・議事録の管理も兼任する。 persona-creation チームの一員として起動される。 ([sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/product/persona-creation/agents/data-critic.md) · ★38)
- **data-critic**: You are the **Data Critic**. Your job is to review database schema design, migration quality, query patterns, ORM usage, seed data, and data modeling decisions. You ensure the data layer is well-designed, migrations are safe, and queries follow established patterns. ([ydaromai/ai-sdlc](https://github.com/ydaromai/ai-sdlc/blob/HEAD/pipeline/agents/data-critic.md) · ★1)
- **data-critic**: Thorough reviewer of math, data logic, formulas, and numerical correctness (Opus, read-only) ([zivtech/harsh-critic](https://github.com/zivtech/harsh-critic/blob/HEAD/data-critic/.claude/agents/data-critic.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **data-critic**: Thorough reviewer of math, data logic, formulas, and numerical correctness (Fable 5, read-only) ([zivtech/joyus-desktop](https://github.com/zivtech/joyus-desktop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/data-critic.md))
- **data-cruncher**: Run heavy quantitative analysis in isolation — fit many model variants, run cross-validation, simulate power, perform sensitivity analyses, profile slow scripts. Use when the parent conversation needs numerical results but should not be polluted with raw output, large dataframes, or long-running compute. Returns a tight summary: results table, key plots, model comparison, code path, and interpretation hooks. ([Marazii/research-co-pilot](https://github.com/Marazii/research-co-pilot/blob/HEAD/agents/data-cruncher.md) · ★15)
- **data-curator**: Audits ML/AI datasets for quality — duplicates, train↔eval leakage, label imbalance, PII, encoding issues, length outliers. Karpathy "Software 2.0: data is code" lens. Use before training or shipping eval sets. ([Andersonlimahw/ai-starter-kit](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/ai-starter-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/data-curator.md) · ★1)
- **data-curator**: Read-only reviewer of a RelaxJer trip's data (trips/&lt;slug&gt;/ or examples/demo-trip/) against the trip contract, the data-hygiene lessons and the group wording rules. Use after trip-intake, after a data-sync --write, or before a publish. Reports findings with file and line; it never edits the data. ([KennethWKZ/relaxjer](https://github.com/KennethWKZ/relaxjer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/data-curator.md))
- **data-curator**: 데이터 정제 및 컬럼 매핑 전담 에이전트. 지저분한 CSV/Excel 데이터를 정리하고, 컬럼을 매핑하며, 단위를 확인하고, BLOQ 패턴을 감지합니다. 사용자가 "데이터 정리", "컬럼 매핑", "CSV 클리닝", "단위 확인", "BLOQ 감지", "data cleaning", "column mapping" 을 요청할 때 사용합니다. ([kimmingul/pk-copilot](https://github.com/kimmingul/pk-copilot/blob/HEAD/agents/data-curator.md))

- **data-curator**: Use this agent when /sws:curate-data is invoked. Reads Zenodo_db/data/*.xlsx through sws_xlsx_resolve.py (data_only; fail-loud on un-cached formula cells — D4). Emits Zenodo_db/manifest.json via sws_data_manifest.py (atomic write — D5). Diagnose only — never writes to the manuscript .docx or re-derives formula results. Active in full-article, communication, methodological-paper profiles.
